                        - heading "Build Overview" [level=2] [ref=e251]
                        - textbox [ref=e258]:
                          - paragraph [ref=e259]:
                            - strong [ref=e260]: The Frenzy Barbarian
                            - text: is a high attack speed, fast paced build that delivers that raw physical damage you look for in a
                            - strong [ref=e261]: Barbarian
                          - paragraph [ref=e262]:
                            - generic [ref=e267]: Frenzy
                            - text: is your main ability and main source of damage. You rely on the new unique
                            - generic [ref=e272]: Hooves of the Mountain God
                            - text: ", the recently updated"
                            - generic [ref=e277]: Paingorger's Gauntlets
                            - text: and
                            - emphasis [ref=e278]: Tempers
                            - text: to make
                            - generic [ref=e283]: Frenzy
                            - text: hit a wide area of monsters. This combination adds to fast clears of big packs of monsters. This planner will also help you solve all your
                            - strong [ref=e284]: Fury Management
                            - text: issues, explain aspects that may be a little confusing and cover what weapons to set your skills to. There are several variations in the planner so read through.
                          - paragraph [ref=e285]:
                            - text: The
                            - strong [ref=e286]: Barbarians
                            - text: this season will all be running some variation of the
                            - generic [ref=e291]: Melted Heart of Selig
                            - text: +
                            - generic [ref=e296]: Endurant Faith
                            - text: or
                            - generic [ref=e301]: Endurant Faith
                            - text: . While this is not a requirement it does make you almost unkillable and the quality of life will go through the roof. Each planner will have variants without it, but this will be your end-game version you're striving for.
                          - paragraph [ref=e302]:
                            - text: Please read through the
                            - link "How it works" [ref=e303] [cursor=pointer]:
                              - /url: /diablo-4/builds/barbarian-frenzy-barb?ws-ngf5-1=activeVariantId%2C98b22aae-fa00-42ac-b6b5-fe0889380ae6#how-it-works-2

                              - text: for this builds set up.
                            - paragraph [ref=e855]:
                              - text: When choosing your
                              - strong [ref=e856]: Barbarian
                              - strong [ref=e857]
                              - emphasis [ref=e858]: Weapon Expertise Technique,
                              - text: you do not have to be using the weapon of the
                              - emphasis [ref=e859]: Weapon Expertise Technique
                              - text: you chose.
                            - paragraph [ref=e860]: "Below is just an Example:"

                                - strong [ref=e866]: Arsenal Selection
                                - text: ":"
                                - strong [ref=e867]: Mace
                                - text: "- Lucky Hit: Chance to gain 5 Fury when hitting an enemy. Double the amount of Fury gained while Berserking and you deal 15(x) Critical Strike Damage while Berserking."

                              - strong [ref=e870]

                              - strong [ref=e871]: Arsenal Selection
                              - text: set to Two-Handed Mace, as well as the

                      - generic [ref=e1061]:
                        - heading "Frequently Asked Questions" [level=2] [ref=e1065]
                        - textbox [ref=e1072]:
                          - heading "Build Overview" [level=3] [ref=e1073]
                          - heading "Arsenal Selection" [level=3] [ref=e1074]
                          - paragraph [ref=e1075]:
                            - text: This build will be running on a
                            - strong [ref=e1076]: Dual Wield
                            - text: set up in order to take advantage of
                            - generic [ref=e1081]: Ramaladni's Magnum Opus
                            - text: combined with
                            - generic [ref=e1086]: Shard of Verathiel
                            - text: .
                          - list [ref=e1087]:
                            - listitem [ref=e1088]:
                              - strong [ref=e1089]: "If you would like to learn how to set your weapon selection watch this video:"
                              - link "How to Select Weapons video" [ref=e1090] [cursor=pointer]:
                                - /url: https://youtu.be/dUuG2CJyAvQ?si=Lz-LY5MK3EehP_CC
                          - heading "Fury Management" [level=3] [ref=e1091]
                          - paragraph [ref=e1092]:
                            - generic [ref=e1097]: Shard of Verathiel
                            - text: will cause
                            - generic [ref=e1102]: Frenzy
                            - text: to cost
                            - strong [ref=e1103]: Fury
                            - text: . That combined with
                            - generic [ref=e1108]: Ramaladni's Magnum Opus
                            - text: can be a strain on your fury. Below is your steps to manage that.
                          - heading "Affixes" [level=4] [ref=e1109]
                          - list [ref=e1110]:
                            - listitem [ref=e1111]:
                              - strong [ref=e1112]: Resource Generation
                              - text: will help increase the amount of
                              - strong [ref=e1113]: Fury
                              - emphasis [ref=e1114]
                              - text: you gain and pairs really well with
                              - generic [ref=e1119]: Rallying Cry
                              - text: . You can
                              - strong [ref=e1120]: Temper
                              - text: this onto your
                              - emphasis [ref=e1121]: Rings
                              - text: and
                              - emphasis [ref=e1122]: Amulet.
                            - listitem [ref=e1123]:
                              - strong [ref=e1124]: Fury Regeneration
                              - text: needs to be added onto the Helmet, Chest piece, Pants and Boots.
                            - listitem [ref=e1125]:
                              - text: Do not spam
                              - generic [ref=e1130]: Frenzy
                              - text: if you are not near monsters if you can't maintain
                              - strong [ref=e1131]: Fury
                              - text: .
                          - heading "Defense (Non-Selig)" [level=4] [ref=e1132]
                          - list [ref=e1133]:
                            - listitem [ref=e1134]:
                              - text: Take all defense nodes inside the
                              - emphasis [ref=e1135]: Paragon
                              - text: board first. All
                              - emphasis [ref=e1136]: Maximum Life
                              - text: ","
                              - emphasis [ref=e1137]: Resistances,
                              - text: and
                              - emphasis [ref=e1138]: Armor
                              - text: nodes.
                            - listitem [ref=e1139]:
                              - text: You can use glyphs like
                              - generic [ref=e1144]: Ire
                              - text: or
                              - generic [ref=e1149]: Territorial
                              - text: to help keep yourself alive.
                            - listitem [ref=e1150]:
                              - text: Make sure all your gear has
                              - emphasis [ref=e1151]: Maximum Life
                              - text: on it and you can then add
                              - emphasis [ref=e1152]: Resistances,
                              - text: and
                              - emphasis [ref=e1153]: Armor
                              - text: as needed.
                          - paragraph [ref=e1154]:
                            - strong [ref=e1155]: "If you want to learn how to set your defenses up, watch this video:"
                            - link "How to avoid getting one-shot video" [ref=e1156] [cursor=pointer]:
                              - /url: https://youtu.be/5IrPJ-H_uLI?si=yDaUSCn8owx0mDWj
                          - heading "Offense" [level=4] [ref=e1157]
                          - paragraph [ref=e1158]:
                            - strong [ref=e1159]: "If you want to learn the basics about Multipliers watch this video:"
                            - link "Additive vs. Multiplicative Damage video" [ref=e1160] [cursor=pointer]:
                              - /url: https://youtu.be/xkaKBMKqPcs?si=kDEk0cc_up4pfUKj
                            - text: .
                          - paragraph [ref=e1161]:
                            - strong [ref=e1162]: Found a Mythic and want to know if it's useable?
                            - link "Is this Mythic usable with my build video?" [ref=e1163] [cursor=pointer]:
                              - /url: https://youtu.be/JGUtZ2TzXso?si=5cgPsJCZ1lJv-3rC
